```diff
+// dropPushLinknames overlays each file of package runtime that pushes a
+// definition into a replaced function (`//go:linkname local P.F`, the
+// runtime defining P's bodyless F: sync.runtime_Semacquire,
+// internal/bytealg.MakeNoZero) with that directive blanked, so that gc links
+// the model's body of F instead of refusing a duplicated definition. The
+// runtime's function stays, unexported and unlinked. Goose is unaffected: a
+// directive is not translated. The file keeps its offsets (the directive
+// becomes spaces), and a file another model already overlays is edited in
+// that overlay.
+func (b *builder) dropPushLinknames() error {
+	targets := make(map[string]bool)
+	for _, pm := range b.models.Packages {
+		for _, n := range pm.Config.Replaces {
+			if n != "*" && !strings.HasPrefix(n, "init.") {
+				targets[pm.PkgPath+"."+n] = true
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	if len(targets) == 0 {
+		return nil
+	}
+	pkgs, err := packages.Load(b.pkgConfig(packages.NeedName|packages.NeedFiles, b.opts), "runtime")
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	for _, pkg := range pkgs {
+		for _, path := range pkg.GoFiles {
+			src, ok := b.models.Overlay[path]
+			if !ok {
+				if src, err = b.source(path); err != nil {
+					return err
+				}
+			}
+			lines := strings.SplitAfter(string(src), "\n")
+			changed := false
+			for i, line := range lines {
+				fields := strings.Fields(line)
+				if len(fields) == 3 && fields[0] == "//go:linkname" && targets[fields[2]] {
+					lines[i] = strings.Repeat(" ", len(strings.TrimRight(line, "\n"))) +
+						line[len(strings.TrimRight(line, "\n")):]
+					changed = true
+				}
+			}
+			if changed {
+				b.models.Overlay[path] = []byte(strings.Join(lines, ""))
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
 type builder struct {
 	modDir, configDir string
 	opts              LoadOptions
@@ -1242,10 +1296,54 @@ func typeString(t types.Type) string {
 	return types.TypeString(t, func(p *types.Package) string { return p.Path() })
 }
 
+// unnamedParams is t with the names of the parameters and results of its
+// function types dropped (in t and in the pointer, slice, array, map and
+// channel types it is built of), which types.Identical ignores too: a model
+// must name a parameter the real bodyless declaration leaves unnamed
+// (`func stopTimer(*Timer) bool`) to use it. Everything else, variadic-ness
+// and type parameters included, is kept.
+func unnamedParams(t types.Type) types.Type {
+	switch t := t.(type) {
+	case *types.Signature:
+		strip := func(tup *types.Tuple) *types.Tuple {
+			vars := make([]*types.Var, tup.Len())
+			for i := range vars {
+				v := tup.At(i)
+				vars[i] = types.NewParam(v.Pos(), v.Pkg(), "", unnamedParams(v.Type()))
+			}
+			return types.NewTuple(vars...)
+		}
+		// fresh type parameters (one may be bound to one signature only),
+		// printed as the originals
+		tparams := func(l *types.TypeParamList) []*types.TypeParam {
+			var ps []*types.TypeParam
+			for i := 0; i < l.Len(); i++ {
+				tp := l.At(i)
+				ps = append(ps, types.NewTypeParam(
+					types.NewTypeName(tp.Obj().Pos(), tp.Obj().Pkg(), tp.Obj().Name(), nil), tp.Constraint()))
+			}
+			return ps
+		}
+		return types.NewSignatureType(nil, tparams(t.RecvTypeParams()), tparams(t.TypeParams()),
+			strip(t.Params()), strip(t.Results()), t.Variadic())
+	case *types.Pointer:
+		return types.NewPointer(unnamedParams(t.Elem()))
+	case *types.Slice:
+		return types.NewSlice(unnamedParams(t.Elem()))
+	case *types.Array:
+		return types.NewArray(unnamedParams(t.Elem()), t.Len())
+	case *types.Map:
+		return types.NewMap(unnamedParams(t.Key()), unnamedParams(t.Elem()))
+	case *types.Chan:
+		return types.NewChan(t.Dir(), unnamedParams(t.Elem()))
+	}
+	return t
+}
```

### goose/util/model_linkname_test.go  (tier 0, translator)

```diff
diff --git a/goose/util/model_linkname_test.go b/goose/util/model_linkname_test.go
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..503aa6ae8
--- /dev/null
+++ b/goose/util/model_linkname_test.go
@@ -0,0 +1,62 @@
+package util
+
+import (
+	"os"
+	"os/exec"
+	"path/filepath"
+	"strings"
+	"testing"
+)
+
+// TestModelRuntimePushLinkname: a model of a function whose definition the
+// runtime pushes (internal/bytealg.MakeNoZero, `//go:linkname
+// bytealg_MakeNoZero internal/bytealg.MakeNoZero` in runtime/slice.go) gets
+// the runtime's directive blanked in the overlay, so that gc links the
+// model's body (and not "duplicated definition of symbol").
+func TestModelRuntimePushLinkname(t *testing.T) {
+	if testing.Short() {
+		t.Skip("rebuilds the runtime (-short)")
+	}
+	root := t.TempDir()
+	writeTree(t, root, map[string]string{
+		"go.mod":                    "module m\n\ngo 1.26\n",
+		"cfg/internal/bytealg.toml": "[model]\ndir = \"model\"\nreplaces = [\"MakeNoZero\"]\n",
+		"model/m.go": "package bytealg\n\n// MODEL: a test model, zeroed, of capacity n+1.\n" +
+			"func MakeNoZero(n int) []byte { return make([]byte, n, n+1) }\n",
+		"main/main.go": "package main\n\nimport \"strings\"\n\nfunc main() {\n" +
+			"\tvar b strings.Builder\n\tb.Grow(5)\n\tprintln(b.Cap())\n}\n",
+	})
+	models, err := buildModels(root, "./main")
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	var slice string
+	for path, src := range models.Overlay {
+		if filepath.Base(path) == "slice.go" && filepath.Base(filepath.Dir(path)) == "runtime" {
+			slice = string(src)
+		}
+	}
+	if slice == "" {
+		t.Fatal("runtime/slice.go is not overlaid")
+	}
+	if strings.Contains(slice, "internal/bytealg.MakeNoZero") {
+		t.Fatal("runtime/slice.go keeps its go:linkname to internal/bytealg.MakeNoZero")
+	}
+	if !strings.Contains(slice, "func bytealg_MakeNoZero(") {
+		t.Fatal("runtime/slice.go lost bytealg_MakeNoZero")
+	}
+	ov, err := models.WriteGoOverlay(LoadOptions{}, filepath.Join(root, "ov"))
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	cmd := exec.Command("go", "run", "-overlay", ov, "./main")
+	cmd.Dir = root
+	cmd.Env = append(os.Environ(), "GOOS=linux", "GOARCH=amd64")
+	out, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("go run: %v\n%s", err, out)
+	}
+	if string(out) != "6\n" {
+		t.Errorf("go run printed %q, want the model's capacity: \"6\\n\"", out)
+	}
+}
```
